# Skills · B · part 79

934 entries, brainstorm-a-feature to brainstorming. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **brainstorm-a-feature**: Turn a problem, a request or a hunch into two or three feature ideas worth a spec. Gathers ideas from four places (the bots that own each area, the owner, customers, and usage data), challenges the premise before any solution is offered, writes at least seven options including "do nothing" and "remove something", argues against the strongest ones, then converges to two or three candidates, each with its evidence, what it adds to the product, its biggest unknown and the cheapest test that would settle it. Use when a task or chat says brainstorm, ideas for, options for, how might we, what shoul… ([ticoteam/tico](https://github.com/ticoteam/tico/tree/HEAD/templates/catalog/_shared/.claude/skills/brainstorm-a-feature) · ★1)
- **brainstorm-agent**: Collaborative exploration specialist for clarifying intent and approach (Phase 0). Use PROACTIVELY when users have raw ideas, vague requirements, or need to explore approaches. Example 1 — User has a raw idea without clear requirements: user: "I want to build an automated data processing pipeline" assistant: "I'll use the brainstorm-agent to explore this idea and clarify requirements." Example 2 — User needs to compare approaches: user: "Should I use Lambda or Cloud Run for this?" assistant: "Let me invoke the brainstorm-agent to explore both approaches with trade-offs." ([asabocinski/bravo-edd](https://github.com/asabocinski/bravo-edd/tree/HEAD/.codex/skills/brainstorm-agent) · ★2)

- **brainstorm-first**: Before starting any non-trivial task (&gt;0.5 day, new feature, new UI page, new integration, refactor spanning &gt;5 files), it is mandatory to ask the requester 3-5 clarifying questions about scope, edge cases, and success criteria — and wait for answers BEFORE planning or coding. ([All-projects-Rust/agent-guardrails](https://github.com/All-projects-Rust/agent-guardrails/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm-first) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **brainstorm-first**: Socratic requirements exploration before writing any code. Use when starting a new feature, project, or design, when a request is ambiguous or underspecified, when someone asks to brainstorm, spec, or think through an approach, or before write-plan on anything non-trivial. Extracts requirements one question at a time, surfaces hidden assumptions and tradeoffs, and ends with a short approved design brief with verifiable success criteria. ([Parallelloop-ai/ai-sdlc](https://github.com/Parallelloop-ai/ai-sdlc/tree/HEAD/plugins/ai-sdlc/skills/brainstorm-first))
- **brainstorm-for-systems**: Brainstorm de sistema que sai com a documentação técnica pronta — força a lente de especificação do `brainstorm`, mantém o glossário durante a conversa, acumula as decisões técnicas como ADRs candidatos e, no portão de consolidação, grava os ADRs confirmados e gera o TRD via `escrever-trd`. ([fabricioveronez/harness-publico](https://github.com/fabricioveronez/harness-publico/tree/HEAD/plugins/spec-driven-development/skills/brainstorm-for-systems) · ★1)
- **brainstorm-forcer**: Research-driven brainstorming controlled phase by phase with durable artifacts. Use for feature design, behavior changes, or system design while brainstorm-forcer is active. ([abdwhb-png/my-pi-setup](https://github.com/abdwhb-png/my-pi-setup/tree/HEAD/agent/extensions/brainstorm-forcer/skills/brainstorm-forcer))
- **brainstorm-front-door**: The anima brainstorm front door (①) — turn a one-line creative spark into a Maya-ready brief bundle (concept doc + Studio Brief + character seeds + handoff) that `python -m pipeline.run --brief &lt;dir&gt;` consumes. Use when Sean brings a new piece idea, a spark, a "what if we made…", or asks to start a brainstorm / concept / new short. USER-INVOKED — this skill runs the room and calls the stage skills; do not invoke it from another skill. ([seanwinslow28/anima](https://github.com/seanwinslow28/anima/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/brainstorm-front-door) · ★2)
- **brainstorm-funnel-tests**: Use when turning a funnel's analytics for a period into recommendations and A/B test ideas: the period's dashboard metrics with one focus area (first step, quiz or paywall), steps whose drop-off exceeds the threshold, and tests grilled in rounds, prototyped as radically different variants and converged to one hypothesis and variant each, rendered as a shareable funnelsgrove PDF. ([The-Solid-Grove/fstack](https://github.com/The-Solid-Grove/fstack/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm-funnel-tests) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **brainstorm-game**: Turn a vague game idea into a 1-page brief (genre, scope, core loop, mechanics) in .summer/GameSoul.md. ([SummerEngine/summer](https://github.com/SummerEngine/summer/tree/HEAD/library/skills/brainstorm-game) · ★109 · 2 copies)
- **brainstorm-gate**: Design-first gate that prevents premature implementation. Use when the task is ambiguous, has multiple valid approaches, or the user hasn't specified how something should work. Forces clarification before any code is written. ([Silex-Research/DontPanic](https://github.com/Silex-Research/DontPanic/tree/HEAD/claude/skills/brainstorm-gate) · ★5)
- **brainstorm-github-pr**: Activate PR brainstorming protocol to analyze and visualize commit history, review threads, and CI pipeline checks using Mermaid diagrams. You MUST load this skill when asked to analyze or brainstorm a Pull Request. ([Cogni-AI-OU/cogni-ai-agent-skills](https://github.com/Cogni-AI-OU/cogni-ai-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/brainstorm-github-pr) · ★4 · 2 copies)
- **brainstorm-goal**: Goal-oriented brainstorm — turn rough ideas into design specs whose §1 is a machine-verifiable Goal, so the plan step can emit a copy-paste /goal + /execute handoff. Use when you want autonomous goal-tracked execution. ([stonelyd/seiraiyu-superwisdom](https://github.com/stonelyd/seiraiyu-superwisdom/tree/HEAD/skills/brainstorm-goal))
- **brainstorm-grill**: The relentless variant of brainstorm. Interrogates a plan or design exhaustively. Walks down every branch of the decision tree, resolves dependencies one by one, and gives a recommended answer for every question. Then converges on an approved design and chains into feature-spec. For a grill that ends at decisions and a brief.md with no feature-spec chain, use grill-me instead. Use when the user wants to stress-test a plan, get grilled on a design, harden an idea against gaps, or says 'grill me', 'grill this', 'interrogate my plan', 'poke holes in this'. ([Kiriketsuki/agenKic-hub](https://github.com/Kiriketsuki/agenKic-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/core/brainstorm-grill))
- **brainstorm-grilling**: Socratic questioning that stress-tests a request before any solution is designed, surfacing the real problem, hidden constraints, and unstated success criteria. Use at the start of a feature or project, before planning. ([LuanNguyen93/Agent-Team](https://github.com/LuanNguyen93/Agent-Team/tree/HEAD/plugins/agent-team/skills/brainstorm-grilling) · ★2)
- **brainstorm-guerrilla-marketing**: Brainstorms low-cost guerrilla marketing ideas for a business, rating each for feasibility, cost, reach and risk, naming the permissions to check, and planning the top three. ([hermes-hq/hodios-dist](https://github.com/hermes-hq/hodios-dist/tree/HEAD/native/codex/.agents/skills/brainstorm-guerrilla-marketing) · ★1 · 5 copies)

- **brainstorm-internal**: The divergence technique CYPRESS applies to itself, with no user in the loop — generating genuinely distinct options against evidence already in hand, stating what would have to be true for each, and naming the one fact that would kill each. Use when a choice is the session's to make and the user has nothing to add to it - filling grill.md §7, assembling an ADR's rejected alternatives, choosing between two implementations that satisfy the same contract, or deciding how to sequence work. - **brainstorm-is-not-a-build**: In an open brainstorm about ideas or ways to make money, pushback or enthusiasm is not a go-ahead. - **brainstorm-issue**: Brainstorme INTERACTIVEMENT une idée ou une issue brute avec Manu, puis dépose la SPEC D'INTENTION résultante dans le corps de l'issue GitHub du repo concerné.
